# Converter quality report: core-real-generated-local (2026-10-17)

1772 results across azw3, mobi. Gap categories and their kinds are defined in `tests/harness/schema.md`; a book can carry several categories.

## Before vs after

Baseline: `tests/corpus/round1-results` (result files of the original writer). Books are paired by sha256 and format; categories are recomputed with the current taxonomy on both sides. A lower count is better in every category row.

## Format: azw3

### 1. Summary

886 books were run for AZW3; 876 (99%) produced an output file and 10 did not. Calibre itself converted 834 of 886. 876 of 876 (100%) converted books pass all three parser gates (mobitool, KindleUnpack, Calibre reader). 13 books carry at least one actionable gap (a book can carry several kinds): 0 have unported Calibre transform gaps (0%), 1 have input repair gaps (8%), 12 have writer bug gaps (92%); 13 category findings in total. Verdict: writer bug gaps affect the most books (92%), so the next effort should fix the KF8/MOBI writer defects, starting with `images-misreferenced`. 776 of 886 books have no actionable gap and were fully evaluated; 9 more books with no gap found were not fully evaluated (harness error, clean refusal or missing invariants); 88 fixed-layout books are out of scope and not counted. Invariants not evaluated (books): text 12, images 12, toc 13, links 12, style 12. Not counted above (category findings): 6 acceptable, 880 informational, 88 out of scope.

### 2. Top gap categories by affected books

| # | category | books | % of books | description | kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `images-misreferenced` | 5 | 1% | Image references in the text point at the wrong image or at none | writer bug |
| 2 | `app-error` | 4 | 0% | App produced no file and the input is not expected to fail | writer bug |
| 3 | `toc-labels` | 3 | 0% | TOC entries whose labels/targets do not match the input | writer bug |
| 4 | `input-repair` | 1 | 0% | App failed or lost text on a malformed input Calibre converted anyway | input repair |

Informational, acceptable, harness and out-of-scope categories are not ranked here (`calibre-header-diff`, `images-resized`, `images-unextractable`, `spine-non-xhtml`, `app-clean-error`, `harness-error`, `fixed-layout-unsupported`), and nothing on a fixed-layout book is; they appear in the per-category breakdown and the per-book table. `text-loss` counts as `input repair` on books whose verdict is error/warning, otherwise as `writer bug`.

## Format: mobi

### 1. Summary

886 books were run for MOBI; 876 (99%) produced an output file and 10 did not. Calibre itself converted 833 of 886. 876 of 876 (100%) converted books pass all three parser gates (mobitool, KindleUnpack, Calibre reader). 304 books carry at least one actionable gap (a book can carry several kinds): 292 have unported Calibre transform gaps (96%), 1 have input repair gaps (0%), 13 have writer bug gaps (4%); 505 category findings in total. Verdict: unported Calibre transform gaps affect the most books (96%), so the next effort should port the missing Calibre transforms, starting with `style-lost`. 485 of 886 books have no actionable gap and were fully evaluated; 9 more books with no gap found were not fully evaluated (harness error, clean refusal or missing invariants); 88 fixed-layout books are out of scope and not counted. Invariants not evaluated (books): text 12, images 12, toc 13, links 12, style 12. Not counted above (category findings): 6 acceptable, 889 informational, 141 out of scope.

### 2. Top gap categories by affected books

| # | category | books | % of books | description | kind |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `style-lost` | 268 | 30% | Style score below 0.5, or the input has CSS rules and the output none | unported Calibre transform |
| 2 | `links-unresolved` | 223 | 25% | Internal links that no longer resolve | unported Calibre transform |
| 3 | `images-misreferenced` | 6 | 1% | Image references in the text point at the wrong image or at none | writer bug |
| 4 | `app-error` | 4 | 0% | App produced no file and the input is not expected to fail | writer bug |
| 5 | `toc-labels` | 3 | 0% | TOC entries whose labels/targets do not match the input | writer bug |

Informational, acceptable, harness and out-of-scope categories are not ranked here (`calibre-header-diff`, `images-resized`, `images-unextractable`, `spine-non-xhtml`, `app-clean-error`, `harness-error`, `fixed-layout-unsupported`), and nothing on a fixed-layout book is; they appear in the per-category breakdown and the per-book table. `text-loss` counts as `input repair` on books whose verdict is error/warning, otherwise as `writer bug`.

### 3. Books most needing a manual look in Kindle Previewer
